the erasure is the walk's own act, the era boundary the first composition is). **The focus raises**: the entry moves to the stack's top, the next composition painting it forward. **The walk prunes**: an entry whose grant the table no longer holds leaves before the walk (a closed window's or a dead owner's surface never holds a slot), and the death notice's reclaim drops the dead owner's surfaces in the same pass. **The pointer's scale is a verb**: `input_info(0)` answers the tablet's axes — the input side of the second seam, the range the shell scales the stream's absolute reports by never a constant (the shell's formula the compositor's own, `(v * mode) / (max + 1)`). **The shell parks before its first frame** (the counter's shape): the desk pass waits for the run's first byte before painting, because a composition now erases the era before it, and the window-era screendump must land on a still display — the beat between the eras is a byte on the merged stream. **Three compiler finds, all fixed, all one root — the image's static memory is shared by every pass, so a static that parks an arena pointer dangles when its builder dies**: ① the const thunks' memos (a `.data` flag/value pair per const, A8.3's tail-call find touched them) parked the builder's heap — the machine's second consumer of the font's memo (the desk pass; the first consumer, the screen pass that built the alphabet and the glyph table, had exited and its arena gone home) read a dead pass's heap and the load page fault named it; the gdb walk that found it also found how the walk misleads (the faulting table's l1 entry was a megapage leaf RWU — the *satp's* table was not the dying pass's; `emo_cur_slot`'s space word named the real root, whose l1 slot was empty); ② the fix is the **const region**: the image heap's top 64 KiB — in the user image the root's arena ends at the region's floor, so no spawn's carve ever hands a child the region (the carve is dead code under the parked hooks) while the kernel profile keeps the root's end at the heap's end, its bare-metal spawn carving live blocks from there and the region built once at startup above the few KiB the root bumps (the split is `init_tail_asm`; the kernel-side shrink first hung every bare-metal spawn example, the carve's block landed fine but the found mechanism stayed open, and the per-profile split is what landed) — `emo_alloc_const` bumping down 32-aligned — **the alignment is load-bearing: the tagged words' low bits are the tag's** (`ori a0, a0, 8`), a block from a merely 16-aligned cursor put the tag bits into the address, the reader's `andi -32` untagged to a different block, and the first boot died of `index out of bounds` and `user heap gone` before its first frame; the const twins of the string, boxing, string-alloc and concat helpers (`emo_string_lit_const`, `emo_box_*_const`, `emo_alloc_string_const`, `emo_string_concat_const`) and the const-aware `box_result` at every emitter box site (eleven raw sites plus the typed-call conversions) plus const bodies always dynamic (numerics as static cells) complete it; ③ the **user profile's `emo_fail` crossed the gate at last** (A7.2's silence, found not fixed): the message rides the write verb whole and the tail takes the heap-death route — which is what said why, when the first const-region boot died before its first frame (`user heap 23 gone` where the old tail's `wfi` had been the only trace; the kernel profile's tail stays the SBI poweroff). **The same fix's second half, found a boot later**: a const body runs dynamic now, and a dynamic body's def value is a pooled record — the thunk's memo took its own claim **after** the statement boundary's drain, which had already released the record, so the memo parked a freelist cell and `examples/processes` (the def-values family) hung their spawns; the thunk's retain precedes the drain now. And the thunk's store unboxes a **Pid** result to the raw pid: `const me = self_pid()` parked the dynamic build's boxed cell and every send to `me` aimed at the pointer. And the const-aware box routing had to stay out of the **def-value thunks** — their bodies emit through `gput` (the module buffer, no frame), the frame-bound `put` answered `riscv: no function frame`, and the def-value family's builds refused until the thunk kept its own `gput` box calls (the arena's helpers, as always there). The split's tail (`init_tail_asm`) is a **called** function of its own — `call` spends `ra`, a leaf `runtime_init` doesn't save, and the tail's first cut read the caller's `t1` (emo_procs) through the call and then clobbered it with the −65544 immediate before storing the root's end — the machine's root faulted at −64960 on its first boot line; both branches name `emo_procs` and the offset in their own registers now. **With every const birth in the region the greedy pass's wire line is `user heap 19 gone` again.** **Found beneath them, fixed (EmoOS T8.13's)**: the display service's rect transfer posted offset 0 — the fullscreen transfers it had only ever served were correct by coincidence, the cursor plane's op-2 rect flushes copied the carve's top-left bytes into the host canvas at the sprite's rectangle, and the uniform backgrounds every earlier golden composited over hid it until the windowed desktop's first drag, whose screendump showed a window twice (once truncated, once at the transfer's offset) — `offset = (y * width + x) * 4`. **The debugging that found the dangling memo, recorded for the next one**: gdb against the machine (`-s -S`, a `file main.elf`, a `hbreak` for anything in the staged user image — a software breakpoint is overwritten by the loader's copy) walked the faulting address's table entries; the walk misleads unless you know **the satp's table is the dying pass's only at a fault** (at a breakpoint inside the fault report it still is, but `emo_cur_slot`'s space word is the ground truth), and a level-1 entry that reads as a valid RWU megapage over the faulting address means the walk succeeds now — the fault happened under an earlier table state or a different satp, so dump the slot record (space at 872, arena at 880, stride 888) before believing a PTE. **The scheduling facts the windowed golden grew**: the pointer's abs/btn events are QMP's `input-send-event` (a display position P is P*32 on x and the first value at or past P*128/3 on y; the left button a `btn` event, BTN_LEFT 272 in the stream); a drag's release is the present that shows where it went (the moves are state alone, the compositor's comp line prints at each composition); the focus's raise is invisible until the next composition, so the shell repaints the chrome it changed and presents; and the desk pass's start park (the counter's shape) exists because a composition erases the era before it. Pins: the fb gate 73 ok (the windows' package checked, its targets built, the window golden's nine lines byte-identical from the interpreter and all five hosted targets, the probe painting a window for the metal); `run.sh` green under both timer transports, three consecutive rounds byte-identical on both (218 lines a boot), the ledger `frames 90 54`; `dune runtest` and `@fmt` green. **Standing**: the surfaces' other roles refused by name until a milestone gives them substance; the shell one process owning two windows — the kill-and-restart acceptance (A9.2) is where the stack's reclamation earns its place; a const body that calls an allocating def still builds in the caller's arena (no such const exists in the tree; the region covers the literal, block, boxing and concat sites, the inventory CHECK.md keeps).
- The kill-and-restart acceptance (emo-desktop A9.2, EmoOS T8.14): **landed (2026-11-03) — a client dies without closing its window, the compositor reclaims its frames and surfaces at the next composition, a reborn client proves the stack whole, and the machine never blinks.** The decisions and the find, in order. **The dead-owner cell** (the comp page's own, +448): the trap shim's three death routes — the fault kill, the heap kill, the exit — write the dead pid there before the reap, inside the death's own masked region (one store, no notices posted, the gate untouched), and the compositor reads and clears the cell at the op-17 walk's head — the one place the service's whole state (the rows, the stack's entries) is quiescent — so `comp_reclaim` gives the dead owner's runs back block by block, empties the rows, and the prune the walk runs anyway takes the surfaces off the stack; the wire names the work only when there was any (`comp reclaim <pid>`, silent when the dead owner held nothing). T8.6's "any other death is the reclaim" was the supervisor's half of the debt and never had its arm; the cell is where it landed instead — inside the service, where the walk cannot race the reclaim. **The address find**: the first cut wrote the cell at `0x87002e08 + 0x48`, the process pages' neighborhood, and landed on the pointer driver's max-x cell — `pointer 512 384` broke; the comp page's +448 is the address, and the routes carry it whole. **The find of the session — the flush wait's Unknown `==`**: the desk era's first composition died between the frame's digest and the comp line, an S-mode load page fault at `emo_eq_deep+0x74` with `tval 0x1200000008` — the pointer notice word (`18 << 32`) plus a payload offset, the machine ending loudly through `emo_fail`. The chain: `run.sh` feeds the pointer's abs park immediately after the desk era's gate byte; the tablet's report becomes the driver's notice — a raw `op << 32` word — and lands in the compositor's mailbox while a flush is in flight; `comp_await_flush` (T8.6's stash discipline) tested `r == 0` on the Unknown receive binding **before** `op_of(r)`, `==` on Unknown lowers to `emo_eq_deep(boxed literal, raw)`, and the raw notice wears the Int64 tag in its low five bits — `eq_deep` walked into it as a cell and loaded its "payload" at `0x1200000008`. The bug is as old as the flush await (T8.6) and the notices (T8.11); no earlier beat ever fed a pointer report inside a flush's window, and the A9.2 ordering did. Three reclaim designs (a gate-post notice to the supervisor, one to the service, a supervisor-side arm) crashed identically before the disassembly settled it: the crash was never the reclaim's mechanism, it was the timing the new beats exposed. The walk that found it: the fault trace's epc → `nm` → `emo_eq_deep` → its four callers (`comp_await_flush` one site, `emo_program` three, `emo_atomic_compare_exchange` dead code) → the call site's shape (`emo_box_i64(0)` then `eq_deep`) → the Emo source's `if r == 0` first. (A stale disassembly in `/tmp` misled once — it showed the pre-fix cell address; regenerate before reading.) **The fix**: op_of dispatches first — `op_of(r) >= 0` is a raw gate word (request, notice) and stashes; the reply test stays on the kernel-cell branch (`op_of < 0`: the kernel's own sends are cells, their pointers' arithmetic shift negative) where every word walked into is a real cell. **The metal discipline this names: a raw word posted beside Emo's own sends must never meet `==` on an Unknown binding — op_of is the only question a raw word answers safely.** The comp service's dead op-256 arm (an earlier design's) is gone, and the stale comments (the supervisor's "any other death is the reclaim", the death routine's) name the cell now. **The client era on the wire**: the parent's spawns gated by its `read()` yields (the win pass's shape); client1 opens a toplevel at (96, 320), pattern `0x57494E00`, declares, paints, presents — `comp 96 320 2102906880`, the client's frame alone — says `client up 2`, and dies by the kill store to `0x20000000` its table maps nothing (`user fault 26 cause 15`, `addr 0x20000000`); the rebirth opens at (512, 320), pattern `0x52454200`, grant 3 — `comp reclaim 26` at its composition's head, `comp 512 320 4174893056` the reborn frame alone (a stuck surface would double the carried sum — the arithmetic proof), `client up 3`, the clean `win_close` and exit answering the parent's wait as `client back`; the desk grants renumbered 4 and 5 behind them. The screendump is the model's: the reborn window's rows where it stands, the dead client's old rect the background — the walk cleared the carve and the surface is off the stack — the sprite parked out of both rects. **The ledger's arithmetic closed exactly**: `frames 104 66` (+14 taken, +12 given: each client's spawn takes its arena run, stack and table block, each `win_new` its four-block run; the dead one's every block home through the fault's reap and the reclaim, the reborn one's through its exit and its close). Pins: `run.sh` green under both timer transports, three consecutive rounds byte-identical on both; the ledger `frames 104 66`; `dune runtest` (340) green with the smoke; `@fmt` green. **Standing**: the surfaces' other roles refused by name until a milestone gives them substance (A9.3's increments); the flush wait's lesson applies to every future raw-word poster (the notices are the only ones today).
- The growth contract's five increments (emo-desktop A9.3, EmoOS T8.15): **landed (2026-11-03) — the roles' substance, the negotiation's fields, the frame callback, the protocol's version, one verb each, M9 complete on both sides of the gate.** The derivation, recorded since the assessment leaves the five to the trackers: the seams M9's window work still owed after A9.1/A9.2 — the third seam's growth (A9.1's standing note: "the surfaces' other roles are refused by name until a milestone gives them substance"), the fourth seam's two fields, the fifth seam's callback and version. **The roles' substance** (verb 19 `win_parent(grant, parent)`, mailbox op 20, route −1020): a popup's or a subsurface's entry takes the parent word (entries 32 → 40 bytes), the walk paints free-standing surfaces in stack order with their children immediately above them at their offsets, then the panels over everything, the blit clipped to the mode (a parent-relative surface rides its parent's moves; the clip's sum is the words actually carried), the prune cascades (an orphaned child leaves with its parent, the passes until still — the binding's one-level rule making one round the whole depth), the move's bounds and the comp line speak the absolute position, a second binding re-points (a surface's role does not change; its parent is not its role), a panel and a toplevel refuse a parent by their role, and the by-number refusal survives on the wire as the second declaration's `role toplevel r4` (`role_name_at` grew the toplevel's name). **The fields** (verbs 20/21): the gate answers itself — the negotiation's shape is the front door's, no service between — recording format (+96) and scale (+104) on the row, the row 96 → 112 bytes (three hardcoded strides and the zero in main.emo with the const), the fresh row's fields zeroed at `win_new`. **The callback** (verb 22 `win_frame()`, mailbox op 21, route −1021): no grant — the callback rides the display's rhythm; the ring four deep at the comp page's +480/+488, a fifth the resource refusal, the composition's completion answering every parker after the presenting caller. **The version** (verb 23): the gate answers 1. **The pass parks past its marker now**: the callback's answer needs a composition from another client, so the win pass self-prints `win 0` (the parent's inline wait retired — a parked pass's exit must not gate an era; the exit code goes unreaped, the exit table's bound) and parks; `cb ok` lands inside the client era, its order against the dying client's lines the scheduler's — a positioned check (after `win 0`, before `client back`), the pointer line's precedent. **The console's one write stage buffer, named by two writers**: the callback's beat parked two console writers at once for the first time in the machine's life, and the single stage buffer printed the last stager's bytes for both — `client up` never reached the wire wearing its own name (the wire showed `cb ok` twice and no client line). The console grew the fs's request-slot shape: four slots on the console's own page (link.ld's `.emo_console` at 0x87009000, above the allocator region's end — never handed out; the sentinels the console service's first act, a NOLOAD page's zeros reading as pending otherwise), the gate's pick refusing a fifth writer (reason 5), **the slot's index NOT riding the route** — the fs's `op + 16 × slot` seats would collide with the compositor's mailbox ops 15 to 21 (the first cut's seat `17` swallowed the composition route: every window present was answered by the console with a slot length — `present r1`, no comp lines; the wire named it) — the route stays −1001 whole and the index sits in a per-pid cell the gate writes and the service reads and clears; a process parks per ecall, one cell per pid is the whole of the race. (The fs's own slot ≥ 1 routes would have hit the same desktop-decode seat — its callers have always serialized, the latent mis-route never fired; the per-pid cell sidesteps it for the console and leaves the fs's shape untouched.) **And the dead-owner cell held one death**: the parked pass's exit landed between the client's fault and the next composition, its death overwriting the client's in the one seat — client1's windows stuck in the stack, the reborn frame's carried sum the doubled arithmetic (`1982832640 = (2102906880 + 4174893056) mod 2³²`; the composition must not drown, so the reclaim of a window-less pid is silent). The dead owners sit in per-pid cells now (256, the comp page's +600, the shim's three writes bounds-checked — the fault/heap/exit routes; a first-seat hazard also retired: +448 had been the stash's second slot since A9.2, a second stashed word would have walked over a dead pid): one write per pid ever — a slot is never reused — so the service's clear cannot race it; a death past the array is the reclaim that never runs, the documented bound. **The grant pins moved with the verbs**: the win pass's three extra buffers (one, one and two blocks) shifted every grant after (the clients' 5 and 6, the desk's 7 and 8), the ledger `frames 108 70` (+4/+4), and the window-present comp lines print before the flush while the client's line prints after its answer — the list's `win shown`/`comp 0 0` order flipped from the T8.6-era listing. Pins: `run.sh` green under both timer transports, three consecutive rounds byte-identical on both; the ledger `frames 108 70`; `dune runtest` (340) green with the smoke; `@fmt` green; `syscalls.md` and `desktop-abi.md` state the verbs and the slots (EN + zh), both READMEs' M9 rows complete — **M9 complete on both sides of the gate**. **Standing**: the fields' values grow under their shapes (a second format, scale 2, are the walk's to honor when a milestone asks); the callback's ring is four deep; the death array's 256 pids are the session's bound.
- The fb backend's family membership (emo-desktop A10.1): **landed (2026-11-03) — the convergence corpus runs through the Elm loop on the pixel surface, the sixth runtime family running what the other five run.** The shape: the shared application required whole from `emo/ui_corpus` (`fb/corpus/`, the package `emo/ui_fb_corpus_golden`, deps ui + ui_fb + ui_fb_canvas + ui_fb_frame + ui_corpus — the digest helper living beside the counter module is the frame golden's own precedent), the Eye over the canvas at the baseline mode, the script `0,0,1,2` the tui and debug entries feed, every frame's digest printed beside `update`'s transitions. The whole node vocabulary the counter never touched — TextInput, Scroll, Image, Spacer, the column's padding and the row's gap — laid out and painted under the loop with no fault: A7.1's box model coverage held. The digests carry the corpus's own arithmetic: `dec 1` and `submit 1` land on the same count as `inc 1` and the same digest. The fb gate stages `emo/ui_corpus` into its registry (the content-named sum growing — every staged registry in the tree restages on the sources' change, so the first run's lock writes are the expected kind) and checks the family beside its siblings, 73 → 90 ok. The metal's leg is unchanged: the same loop on the carve since A8.3, `run.sh`'s screen pass. Pins: `fb/check.sh` 90 ok under the opam env; `dune runtest` untouched (no src change); the EmoUI README names the family (EN + zh).
- The dock and the desktop shell (emo-desktop A10.2): **landed (2026-11-03) — `emo_desktop`, the shell's chrome as a package, and the machine's desktop wearing a dock.** The decisions, in order. **The package** is the flat name the placement round decided (`emo_desktop`, `fb/desktop/`), targets all six, deps `emo/ui_fb` alone; the dock paints direct (fills and glyphs through the `Surface`, no tree — the window package's arena reason), the strip a panel surface at the display's bottom edge, the role A9.3 gave substance — the walk's topmost, no window over it. **The press policy is a pure def** (`press(x, y, w, h, count)` → the tile or −1), the shell's pointer handler asking it before the window hit test: a strip press names a tile, the tile names its app, `win_focus` raises it, the dock repaints with the tile lit; a strip press never starts a drag (the drag's grab conditions grew the below-the-strip guard — a window dragged to the bottom edge must not mistake a dock press for its own bar), and no window beneath is consulted. **The hosted twin paints the dock buffer's own size** (1024×40, not the mode): the digest the golden pins is the digest the screendump's rect fold meets — the same identity the window golden's frames carry — in the three states the desk era lives (none focused, alpha lit, beta) with the policy's probes beside; the fb gate stages `emo_desktop` and checks the family (90 → 107 ok). **The desk pass wears the dock**: the panel surface beside its two windows, the initial paint unlit, the repaints riding the one focus-change block, the three digest lines pinned by a corpus-file loop seeded past the desk era's start (the pointer-line positioning's shape), `desk_is` growing the strip's rectangle — skipped in the walk, folded to the corpus's digest, the fold generalized to per-rect dimensions — and the beta focus beat moved from a press inside the window to a press on beta's tile (`abs 2048 31979`), the drag's bar press after it finding beta already focused. The ledger `frames 111 73` (the dock's three blocks, home at its close); the smoke grew `emo_desktop`'s staging and `DOCK_EXPECTED` (the first run named the missing file, the loud end the corpus discipline wants). Pins: `run.sh` green under both timer transports, three consecutive rounds byte-identical on both; `fb/check.sh` 107 ok; `dune runtest` and `@fmt` green.
- The first apps (emo-desktop A10.3): **landed (2026-11-03) — the counter, the file browser and the editor as corpus packages (`emo/ui_apps`), dual-run: the hosted families' bytes and the machine's three app passes, one discipline both seats.** The decisions, in order. **One family package, three submodules**: `fb/apps/` is `emo/ui_apps` (targets all six, deps `emo/ui` alone), the front door `ui_apps.emo` naming the discipline, the apps `counter`/`files`/`editor` reached as `ui_apps.counter.view` — the `ui_fb_frame.counter` shape; a require binds the package's *entry* (`main.emo` or the short name's file), which is why the family has a front door at all. Each app is a corpus package in `emo/ui_corpus`'s: portable defs, nothing platform-naming, `update` saying each transition, the tags registered in the view's own order (add/sub; down/up/open; put_a/put_b/newline) — the browser's listing the model's data (the seed names the desktop's own three sources; the disk's answer is M11's), the editor's cursor row the put's target. The trees are the metal's measured budget — the sessions sized to the pass's 128 KiB arena (A8.3's per-event count). **The metal leg is three passes**, each a window of its own by `win_new` and the Elm loop over the window buffer's surface (the desk pass's `Win`), `AppKeys` the byte table whose tag is an index and `q` the loop's end, spawned after the shell's quit one at a time, the exit answering the parent's wait (`app <name> 0`, the golden's own closing line — the two seats print the same bytes). **The user profile's print was silent — fixed**: `println` lowered for riscv64 since ever, but a user pass's bytes fell into the legacy SBI ecall whose `a0` the gate reads as a buffer address and refuses — the silence A7.2's first frame found and left (`emo_fail`'s announce unheard since; the first app session's frames moved and its words didn't, which named it). The user boot stub now parks the console hook (T3.5's own mechanism): every byte the runtime prints rides the gate's write verb from a one-byte scratch in the image's .bss. **The scratch's first home was wrong**: `.emo_ucall` — the cells page — is not a segment, and the gate's `check_write` admits only the caller's own; the byte died at the range check and the wire stayed silent until the scratch moved into the .bss, the writable segment the check admits. The gate's copy runs interrupt-masked, so no other pass's byte lands between the store and the read; one byte per crossing is the whole of the protocol. **`ArrayBuilder` is not lowered on riscv64 — found-not-fixed, the compiler track's**: the browser's and editor's first drafts grew their arrays with it and the emitter refuses by name; the apps went to literals of their own fixed shapes (the seed catalog and the two-row buffer are fixed data). **The pins**: the hosted golden at the app window's own size (384×128), the sessions' bytes `expected.txt`, all five hosted targets the same; `run.sh`'s ordered list carrying the three eras' transitions and exits (the wire's own words — `add 1`, `sel files.emo`, `put b`, `app editor 0` — the browser's seed names renamed off the shell's `ls` answers after `hello.txt` arrived out of order), the ten frames pinned by the corpus-file scan seeded past the desk's exit and ending before the editor's (the repeated number — the counter's sub returning its mount's frame — the search past the last, the ordered list's first-occurrence scan unable to carry a twice-printed digest), `app_is` folding each app's rectangle at the mount and the last frame with the pointer parked off every app's place; the ledger `frames 132 91`; `run.sh` green under both timer transports, three consecutive rounds byte-identical on both; `fb/check.sh` 130 ok (the three apps' type-checks and riscv64 lowerings pinned beside the goldens); `dune runtest` and `@fmt` green. **With this M10 is complete.**

## The remaining work — one list (recorded 2026-11-05)

Every open task in one place, mirrored from the tracker it belongs
to; an item is checked off here the day its tracker entry is. The
compiler's standing debts carry no plan step yet — they are listed
here so they cannot drift, and enter `docs/TASKS.md` when a step
claims them.

**EmoOS — [Step 8's open tail](projects/emo-os/docs/TASKS.md),
closed 2026-11-06:**

- [x] **T8.19** — the desktop shell era, kernel side: `parent_for`,
  the exit event ring, the death kick, the comp verbs' slot staging
  (closing the `comp_args` race). Landed 2026-11-06 from the archived

## 2026-11-11 — The scratch gets its own mebibyte; the golden re-pins (the arena track's close)

- The console renderer's death was never a hang and never the arena:
  the counter child died in the doomed branch (`user fault 33 cause
  13` reading 0x80000000) because every token cell in `buf`
  (0x87200000) already held a coverage word. The boot pass IS the
  desktop (the top level's last statement, `desktop()` at 3655), and
  the desktop's eager strike construction (emo-desktop A17.4) bumps in
  the boot pass's own arena — the image's static heap from
  `emo_heap_start` (0x87180000) — where 385 KiB of face assembly,
  builder backings and raw arrays walked straight through the scratch
  page's token cells at buf+2048. The monitor's physical-memory
  snapshot made it visible (`pmemsave` of the image's whole span at
  four beats: the cells read as vertex-adjacent words from the first
  prompt on; the walk over the bump's count headers ended at the first
  raw array's −1 header, the boot pass's heap holding a whole strike's
  worth of −1-marked arrays). The retired ink's tables lived in the
  const region at the heap's top, which is why the walk never happened
  before A17.4.
- The fix is structural: the user and app profiles' `.bss` split the
  image heap — a mebibyte reservation (`emo_scratch_base`) for the raw
  scratch the packages' `buf` literals name, the bump's own range
  starting above it (`emo_heap_start` 0x87180000→0x87280000; the app
  profile's 2 MiB total and every image's span and const region
  unchanged, the kernel profile's single heap untouched). The counter
  pass now renders its frames (`EmoUI on EmoOS`, `count 001`, the quit
  key home) and the golden runs whole, both transports.
- The golden re-pinned from a dual-transport-verified pair: 48 comp
  sums harvested by replaying the awaits' and the order list's own
  walks on the new transcripts (40 moved, 8 unmoved — the no-glyph
  compositions kept their sums, and each window's slide kept a
  constant old→new delta, the mapping's cross-check), the icons'
  refresh frame 624477878, the ledger `frames 980 782`. Three rounds
  on the re-pinned file, both transports, green.
- The spinner's budget story corrected: the loop's machine code is the
  baseline compiler's instruction for instruction; the pace is its
  page placement under TCG — translation blocks chain within a page,
  so a loop whose bytes straddle a 4 KiB boundary pays the block
  lookup every iteration. Measured: 18 s with the loop sitting inside
  one page (the baseline-compiled image embedded in the same kernel),
  167.8–170.9 s straddling (this build's, six boots). The budget is
  3600 rounds (360 s), twice the straddle. Found-not-fixed: the
  placement follows the runtime's size, so the pace flips with any
  shift of the code; the budget now covers both cases.
- One interleave to record, inside the launch era's own race class:
  the REPL's prompt after a slot app's `exec` lands within a line of
  the app's first slide — the prompt's position among the launch's
  lines moved by one between rounds (every pinned line and value
  identical; run.sh passes three rounds). A deterministic ordering
  (prompt printed before the desktop's own repaints, same-writer
  ordering) is available if whole-transcript byte identity is wanted
  again; docs/shell.md's pins note now says what holds.

## 2026-11-10 — The heap-gone was a disguise; the raw world lands (the arena track)

- The metal bring-up's blocker was never the arena. The bring-up's
  `user heap 23 gone` rode the gate's verb 5, which `emo_fail` shares
  with the allocator's exhaustion: the screen pass's strike returned
  `(R.error, "glyph 2's ink does not fit the strike's 24")`, `mark()`
  ate the message, and `sholder.get(0)` on the empty holder died the
  heap death — with 1698 of its 2048 KiB free. The riscv64 emitter's
  representation rule was the disease: `emo_builder_get` answers the
  tagged word its backing holds, the checker's element pin died with
  the scope it pinned in, and a native frame's typed consumers trusted
  rule-1 — `quad_flat` did its ink math on pointers. The region's own
  eyes killed the guesses: `frames.dump` at boot and at every death
  (the boot's 991 blocks; the deaths' 933 free with a 618-block run),
  `take_run`/`take` refusals that name their numbers, the heap report
  carrying the failing message (`emo_fail` parks its pair in s2/s3)
  beside the arena's remainder.
- The raw world: the checker's builder pin sticks past the loop that
  pinned it (`keep_builder_pins`); the builder cell carries a mark and
  the runtime grew `push_i64`/`get_i64` — an empty builder turns raw,
  a started tagged builder boxes, a raw builder refuses tagged loudly;
  `to_array` on a raw builder copies verbatim under a header of −1,
  and the consumers normalize: `emo_arr_tagged` at the equality's and
  display's entries, the typed index read adaptive in both frames (a
  gradual element boxes before the claim), `emo_copy_deep` and
  `emo_copy_out` carrying the mark through the mailbox. A runtime
  error can no longer dress as `heap gone`.
- The arena runs split by class: the services keep 4 blocks, a user
  pass's spawn sets its own run through `arena_len_cell` and the
  process service judges the map's largest run after the stack is out
  — the count alone once passed a shattered region to the runtime's
  loud end. The const region sizes itself from the image's counted
  const demand (hello 64→121 KiB); `screen_flag` moves out of the
  claimed bitmap it had been buried in; the exec unwind stops giving a
  frame to the block give.
- Measured: the strike construction 2039 KiB → 793–862 KiB at the
  first present; counter, files and editor exec, run and exit 0 on the
  metal, every app digest byte-identical with the hosted goldens, the
  shell REPL's write/cat/rm round whole. The golden's comp and frame
  pins await the harvest re-pin (the tree's run.sh carries the relaxed
  form), the console renderer's exec the one open death, and the
  spinner's budget rides the host's TCG weather. The four golden-dir
  locks ride the gate staging's own refresh.

## 2026-11-10 — The seam migrates, the pixel pipeline retires (emo-desktop A17.4)

- `ink`'s strike widens from a table index to a `Strike` — the verb's
  shape the one it has had since A8.1, one interface call a glyph —
  and the threading reaches everything the core measures, places or
  paints: `measure`/`measure_column`/`measure_row`, `place` and its
  two containers, `paint`, `text`, `text_width`, `line_height`; the
  `Fb` backend holds the strike its tree paints in. Every caller
  names its size: the goldens and the corpus drivers build one
  interface strike each at their boot; the three app images hold
  theirs in the session's locals; the desktop's carve pass — the pass
  whose own comment said "no class, no interface, no arena spent" —
  threads the object as the desk defs' first parameter, the reading
  amended: a reference argument boxes one cell a call, a handful a
  repaint, and the walk's arithmetic stays the raw world's.
- The metal carries the whole seam now. The user image — hello's
  session, the desk pass, the window shell's menus, the seat's press
  policy — lowers with the strikes aboard, as do the three app images
  and the probe; the winbuf's `carve_ink` walks the slot words'
  row-major bytes the hosted canvas walks, and the `Keys` seat and
  the menus thread the strike beside the surface. The raise-free
  discipline reached the boot roots: a helper that cannot refuse
  quietly answers the holder pattern — build the strike, case the
  tuple, push, read, the unprefixed read a runtime error the image
  dies loudly on — and the goldens took the same shape, so the
  bytecode targets build their mains whole again.
- **D11 amended**: `font.emo` and `glyphs/gen.py` retired (moved to
  the protected scratch per the deletion rule), but `coverage.txt`
  stays — the retirement reached the generator and found the advance
  cross-check's other side and the audit's data living in the grid;
  regenerable by `rasterize.py` in one command, compiled into
  nothing. The gate's drift checks are now two: the faces' outlines
  and the reference's raster pins.
- The goldens regenerate once, pixels moved by the runtime strikes'
  own coverage (the digest lines keep their shapes: the frame's four,
  the window shell's nine, the dock's ten, the corpus's nine, the
  first apps' twenty), and every package lock under `fb/` moves with
  the package's content — the locks the app images and the user image
  carry too.

## 2026-11-10 — The seam migrates, the pixel pipeline retires (emo-desktop A17.4)

- The seam lands hosted-complete: `ink`'s strike widens from a table
  index to a `Strike`, the threading reaches `measure`/`place`/`paint`
  and the `Fb` backend, every caller names its size, the goldens and
  the corpus and the app images build one interface strike each, and
  the type golden's 288 raster pins stay byte-identical through the
  whole reworking (the reference's 288-pin check and the faces'
  outline drift check both green). `font.emo` and `glyphs/gen.py`
  retire; `coverage.txt` stays as the advance cross-check's other
  side and the audit's data (D11 amended).
- **The metal bring-up stays open, found-not-fixed**: the strike
  construction in the exec'd screen pass dies of heap exhaustion on
  the machine — the pass's early marks print, then the loud
  `user heap 23 gone` — while the interpreter strikes the same code
  correctly. The chase crossed three layers: the store-refactored
  walk broke the ocaml target too (the emitter lowers a sum computed
  at a typed call site, or inside a while body, to the dynamic
  convention — `Emo_runtime.add v_s (!v_j)` where the typed
  signature wants int64s), so `raster.emo`'s raster_glyph reverted to
  the array-based walk that every target and the machine itself had
  already verified, and the construction's arena need moved to the
  lever instead (arena_run to 32 blocks, 2 MiB, the region's ~500
  carrying it beside every resident). The machine's remaining death
  is the kernel-side arena interaction (the spawn's mapped run, the
  take_run granularity, the heap-gone's exact site) — the EmoOS
  track's to instrument with its own eyes. The fence: the fb tree's
  bytes are the hosted state's; the run.sh rounds resume when the
  kernel side answers. The debug marks came out of hello's smoke; the
  images rebuilt clean.

## 2026-11-10 — The rasterizer (emo-desktop A17.3)

- The algorithm's source of truth lands as `glyphs/reference.py`
  (stdlib only), ported to `raster.emo` to match byte for byte: 26.6
  throughout, every division truncating (Emo's own semantics, the
  reference mirroring it), de Casteljau flattening while the second
  difference exceeds one pixel (the deviation bound is a quarter of
  that difference), and the area accumulation as one closed form per
  touched column — the clamp's antiderivative, doubled, `G2(v)` = 0 |
  v² | 128v − 4096, answering `H·(G2(xb − 64c) − G2(xa − 64c)) / D`
  with the strip for the columns left of the piece falling out of the
  same formula, no column splitting, no interpolation beyond the
  row-boundary x's (truncating, a subpixel unit of dust a crossing).
  The sweep runs right to left over `cells[c] + 128·cover_suffix[c]`
  and rounds `min(255, (|A2| + 16) >> 5)`. The algorithm's full
  spelling is the design's (typeface.md, amended where the prose had
  drifted from what the code does).
- The type golden grows 288 raster lines — a glyph per strike, the
  advance, the ink box and a rolling digest over the ink's coverage
  bytes — and `reference.py --check` recomputes them from the
  generator's own outlines and matches the Emo strike byte for byte.
  The two implementations read different roads to the same data (the
  reference takes `outlines.py`'s absolute coordinates, the Emo side
  decodes the face modules' packed words), so the agreement also
  proves the packed decode. The strike rasterizes eagerly at
  construction and fills meta word B; an ink beyond `max_dim` refuses
  through the error convention, as the design's strictness asked.
- Three findings:
  - **The generator's vertex fields were 26 bits, not the documented
    28.** `0x3FFFFFF` is 2²⁶ − 1: negative deltas were two's-
    complemented at the wrong width and every face module carried
    corrupted vertices for any glyph with a negative-going edge — the
    reference never noticed (it reads Python objects), the Emo
    rasterizer's bounding box exploded. The mask corrected to
    `0xFFFFFFF`, the faces regenerated (their parse digests moved),
    the advance cross-check unchanged.
  - **A digest over slot words overflows Int64** — a word packs eight
    coverage bytes and reaches 2⁶⁴ − 1 — so the raster digest folds
    the bytes, which stay 0..255 on every target.
  - **The builder element-type pinning**: `ArrayBuilder[Int64]`
    annotations hold in parameter, field and return positions, but a
    local binding settles only through a static push on the binding
    itself — pushes made inside another def leave `get` answering
    Unknown, and the c target refuses bitwise operators on Unknown
    operands (the instrumented refusal named `left ty Unknown, left
    var mnx`). `glyph_edges` builds and `to_array`s its edges behind
    its own typed return; the sweep indexes the plain array. And
    `ArrayBuilder[Int64].new()` is not a construct — the constructor
    is bare, the element type riding annotations and pins only.
- The FreeType audit ran once, box-aligned (the grid's pixels are
  hinted, the reference is not): the shapes agree, ink boxes move by
  a pixel, stems read a level or two heavier, ink mass −6..−12% —
  hinting's signature, not the algorithm's. A dev report, never a
  gate. The gate gains `reference.py --check` beside the two
  generator drift checks.

## 2026-11-10 — The parser and the Strike (emo-desktop A17.2)

- `raster.emo` joins `emo/ui_fb` as the parser and the Strike's home:
  a face module read whole into a `Face` (the 3,383 and 2,973 outline
  words and the bases copied once, at boot), and `strike(f, px)` —
  the metrics by the pinned formulas (ascent and descent by ceiling,
  the advance half-up, the line box rounded up to eight for
  `max_dim`), the coverage slots dense over the ASCII table (72 words
  a slot at 16px, 128 at 24px), two meta words a glyph (slot base and
  advance; the ink box waits for A17.3). The parse readers
  (`advance_fu`, `points`, `contours`, `contour_end`, the vertex's
  flag and signed deltas) and two rolling digests stand beside, the
  golden's pins.
- Three findings, each a wall the metal named:
  - **The design's "first use rasterizes" amended to eager.** The
    language's arrays are immutable values and its mutable containers
    (Box, List, Map) hold no indexed word storage a blit could read
    at `font.emo`'s shape — so the strike rasterizes at construction
    into a plain `Array[Int64]` its init stores. Boot-time work
    either way; the blit reads the same words every frame.
  - **The raise is not the metal's.** The riscv64 target does not
    lower raise, and the compiler pulls every module of a package
    into its builds (there is no unreachable module) — one `raise`
    in `raster.emo` failed all seven riscv64 probe builds. The
    strike's refusal is the error convention instead:
    `(R.ok, the strike)` or `(R.error, why)`, the syscall package's
    own shape, a value the caller matches.
  - **The constructor ceiling.** The metal refuses a constructor of
    more than seven parameters (`emo_riscv.ml`); the Strike's init
    carried eight, and `line` became derived (ascent + descent).
- The golden (`fb/type/`) pins: the metrics reproducing the grid's
  three strikes exactly (Inter 16/4/20 and 24/6/30, JetBrains Mono
  17/5/22 — the ceiling formula's own confirmation), twelve sample
  advances matching the grid glyph for glyph (space 5/7/10, A 11/17/10,
  f 6/9/10, i 4/6/10 across the three strikes), the parse slices (a
  space's empty drawing, an l's four points in one contour, an o's
  forty-eight in two, a B's fifty-two in three, a vertex's fields
  read back), the zero-size refusal, and two digests — bases then
  outline words, advances per strike — folding `h·31 + w mod
  1000000007`, overflow-free on Int64. Byte-identical on ocaml, c and
  typescript; the golden carries no surface, so wasm and beam refuse
  at build time (the package's interface calls have no implementor in
  its program) and the gate pins both refusals the way the 48x32
  screen's wasm one is pinned.
- The metal lowers the package with the outlines and the Strike
  aboard: all eight riscv64 probe lines green (the user programs, the
  three app images, the window buffer). Every package lock under
  `fb/` regenerated — the package's content hash moved with the three
  new modules, and `apps/spin`'s empty lock (no deps to pin) restored
  byte-identical.

## 2026-11-10 — The type at any size opens (emo-desktop A17.1)

- The faces stop being three baked strikes and start being outlines:
  `glyphs/outlines.py` reads the pinned Inter 4.1 and JetBrains Mono
  2.304 and writes `face_inter.emo` (3,383 outline words, 8 chunks)
  and `face_mono.emo` (2,973, 7) against the 4,269 words of the three
  pixel strikes they retire in A17.4. What the OS-side parser never
  sees, the generator resolved: the cmap (format 4 or 12; every ASCII
  code point present or refusal), the implied on-curve midpoints
  (floored, each contour rotated to start on one), composites
  (translation applied; scale or point-index anchor refused by name).
  Face format 1 is pinned in
  `projects/emo-desktop/docs/typeface.md` (EN + zh-CN): per glyph the
  advance in font units, the vertex and contour counts, the contour
  ends four 16-bit fields to a word, and one word per vertex — the
  on-curve flag in the top byte, the signed deltas in two 28-bit
  fields.
- The advance cross-check runs in both generator modes against the
  grid FreeType rendered: exact rational round-half-up; a one-pixel
  difference is FreeType's own hinting and is reported (`5` at 16px
  and `T` at 24px on Inter; JetBrains Mono 95/95 exact), two or more
  refuse. The metrics' scale is pinned as ceiling — the one rounding
  the grid confirms (Inter 16's ascent 1984·16/2048 = 15.5 → 16, 24's
  23.25 → 24; every ascent, descent and `.notdef` advance of the
  three strikes reproduces exactly).
- The find: the fb gate's wasm goldens had been broken on develop
  since step 56's seventeen-import table landed — the hand-rolled
  `emo-ui/wasm-runner.mjs` (untouched since T48.6) supplies three
  imports, every module links against seventeen, and each golden died
  at instantiate. The gate runs its wasm artifacts under the
  canonical host the compiler carries
  (`src/emo_codegen/wasm/wasm_host.mjs`) now, which cannot drift.
  The same staleness stands in emo-ui's own gates (`check.sh`,
  `corpus/check.sh`, `ssr/check.sh`) — unrepaired, the emo-ui track's
  to sweep.
- Pins: the fb gate green — 152 checks, the faces' drift check among
  them, the goldens byte-identical on the five hosted targets (the
  beam legs minutes-slow at 1080p and correct), the riscv64 probe
  lowering the package with the face modules aboard.

## 2026-11-09 — The wasm follow-ups: entropy, the baseline, the second engine (T56.7–T56.9)

- The entropy refusal died with its premise: T55.7 kept `rand` off
  wasm because "the wasm host supplies no entropy", and step 56's
  canonical host *is* a wasm host with Node behind it. `rand_bytes`
  joins the import table — `(i64 n) -> (tag, size)`, one `fetch`
  serving both branches (the payload is the entropy on ok, the reason
  text on error) — the host answers from Node's CSPRNG (the
  typescript target's own source), `rand` declares `wasm`, and
  `rand_demo` rides the wasm golden list byte-identical (its entropy
  lines are host-independent: lengths, draw-divergence, zero).
- The performance baseline landed: `benchmarks/run.sh` grows the wasm
  rows (module execution only — the driver reuses the canonical
  host's exported import factory and times fresh instantiations of a
  once-compiled module). The numbers, against the same run's c
  target: fib(30) **38 ms vs 39 — parity** (the box churn hides
  behind call overhead); the straight-line loops **172/178 ms vs
  28/36, ~5–6×**; the Bytes scan **983 vs 247, ~4×**; the json-ish
  scan sub-millisecond. The step-16 recorded follow-up — Int
  unboxing — is **declined on the evidence, the reopen trigger
  named**: a real workload whose profile shows box churn dominating.
  The argument lives in the step plan. The `http_echo` bench, still
  calling the pre-M10 `net.listen` shape (the bench set had not been
  re-run since 2026-10-11), was repaired on the way.
- The second engine vouches for the shape: `devtools/wasmtime-smoke.sh`
  feeds every wasm golden (49 examples) to the wasmtime CLI's
  compiler — cranelift's validator checks the whole module against an
  independent implementation: **49/49 validated, zero deltas**. Node
  stays the execution host; the wasmtime CLI cannot supply a non-WASI
  module's custom imports (the machine has no Rust toolchain — the
  canonical host's Rust-driver port is the named follow-up if
  execution parity ever earns it).
- Pins: `dune runtest` green, `dune build @fmt` green;
  `benchmarks/results.md` re-recorded in full by the extended harness
  (five wasm rows); `devtools/wasmtime-smoke.sh` 49/49.

## 2026-11-08 — File IO lands on the wasm target (step 56, M22)

- The wasm target's largest capability gap closed: `file`, `os` (the
  fd, directory, and working-directory half), and `bufio` now declare
  `wasm` and answer byte-for-byte what the interpreter and `c` answer.
  The import table grew from three to seventeen — `file_read`,
  `file_write`, `fetch`, `os_open`, `os_read`, `os_write`, `os_close`,
  `os_list_dir`, `os_mkdir`, `os_rmdir`, `os_unlink`, `os_rename`,
  `os_getcwd`, `os_chdir` — each a syscall that stashes its answer for
  the guest's `fetch`, the reasons the host formats whole against a
  Node error-code → strerror table that spells them exactly as the c
  runtime's `emo_os_fail` does. `list_dir` rides a NUL-separated name
  stream the guest splits with the existing `str_split`, byte-order
  sorted host-side. The process half raises at the call naming the
  capability (the interpreter's run-time-refusal precedent; a
  compile-time refusal would have killed the whole package, whose own
  bodies call those names). The canonical host
  (`src/emo_codegen/wasm/wasm_host.mjs`) rides the compiler as
  generated data; `emo run --target wasm` writes it beside the
  artifact and runs under Node, the riscv64 arm's shape; the golden
  tests' inline runner is deleted for it. `examples/os_fs_demo` walks
  the fd and directory half on interpreter/c/wasm byte-identically;
  `os_demo` (it forks) stays a c golden. New wasm goldens: `file_read`,
  `bufio_demo`, `os_fs_demo`, plus scratch probes for the file
  syscalls and the fork refusal. All lockfiles pinning the changed
  manifests regenerated.
- **Two wasm-runtime bugs found and fixed, both invisible until
  something needed two live bump buffers at once** (every earlier
  consumer — `println`, the float scratch — read its buffer before the
  next allocation): the bump cursor **never moved** — `write_bytes`'s
  copy loop sat directly in its enclosing block and the exit branch
  (`br_if 1`) jumped past the cursor advance, dead code since the
  backend landed (found 2026-11-08, T56.1, the loop now wears its own
  block); and the grow delta **paged the length alone** — `(len +
  65535) >> 16 - memory.size` goes negative once the cursor rides
  along, so past the first page boundary `memory.grow` failed, its
  `-1` was dropped, and the copy stored out of bounds — `plot_demo`'s
  second SVG caught it, the delta now pages `end` (found 2026-11-08,
  T56.1). The bump base moved to 65536 so page 0 stays the host's and
  the `float_str` scratch at 60000 can never be overrun.
- Pre-existing test-suite landmine fixed while wiring the goldens:
  the CLI tests' fixture root (`$TMPDIR/emo-cli-test-fixtures`) was
  assumed to exist — nothing created it, and a wiped temp directory
  took every scratch-writing test with it (found 2026-11-08, T56.4).
  It is created where the path is defined.
- Pins: `dune runtest` green — 376 tests in the CLI group alone, zero
  failures; `dune build @fmt` green (ocamlformat over the three
  touched OCaml files); `emo run --target wasm` byte-identical with
  `expected.txt` on `os_fs_demo`, `file_read`, and `bufio_demo`;
  `emo build --target wasm` still emits exactly `.wasm` + `.wat`.

## 2026-11-08 — The window's own menu (emo-desktop M16, EmoOS T10.2–T10.4)

- **The bar's chrome becomes live.** The three traffic lights the
  A14.5 title bar painted were decoration — hit-tested by no one, the
  bar's press "no one's" in the shell. M16 gives the bar one round
  button and the dropdown it opens: Maximize, Minimize, Quit. The
  architecture the constraints picked: the gate's owner checks make a
  window's grant the only legal caller of its own verbs, so the menu
  lives once in the seat the three apps share (`fb/winbuf`), not in
  the shell; the popup rides the roles' own vocabulary (role 1,
  `win_parent`); the pointer's position reaches the app as the
  **press-at** (T10.2) — a second ring word behind every left-button
  press, the position the axes last named packed raw — because
  pointer-move fanout would race the panel's exemption. Menu language:
  English (the system's face is ASCII; a Chinese menu is six new
  16×16 glyphs — decidable later, the pipeline ready).
- **The kernel's half.** T10.2: the press-at, the press's own
  composition (the raise's marks now `comp_raise_mark`, shared with
  op 15), and the **fold** — a window that loses the focus loses its
  popups with it. T10.3: the hidden word beside the painted records,
  `win_minimize` (verb 25) and `win_desk_open(pid)` (verb 26, the
  panel the authority — the desktop names its apps by pid), the walk
  skipping a hidden toplevel's whole tree. T10.4: `win_scale` accepts
  the whole numbers — the recorded seam's values grown, the scaled
  blit's word pairs doubling or gathering across a straddle, the
  mask's arcs at the carried size; the win pass's strictness beat
  flips (`scale 2 ok`, `scale 0 r4`).
- **The finds.** (1) The desk_open's comp line read the entry's
  position after the raise — the raised-from slot's stale words (the
  dock's own coordinates printed in its place); captured before the
  raise now. (2) `comp_stack_add` never cleared a reused slot's
  hidden word — a shifted-down minimized entry's flag could ride a
  new surface's slot; cleared. (3) The seat's `next` rewrite had the
  quit sentinel inside the loop's own `done < 0` condition — the
  session parked on the stream forever, the whole machine healthy
  around it; five instrumented rounds to see what the structure said
  at first glance. (4) **The exit event waits for the machine's next
  input event when the desktop goes fully silent**: the sink's last
  present ends, the close and the exit land, the exit ring holds the
  news and the shell's reader sits served-parked on the empty stream
  with its hold seat cleared — the ring's `exit_event_for_held` finds
  no seat named, and the event delivers only when the next event
  wakes the serve (its own reader's `exit_event_take` on the resumed
  pass). Every pre-A16 beat's motion masked it; the menu-quit's quiet
  beat exposed it, five more rounds to pin down, and the golden's
  beat texture now does what a user's hand does — the pointer back to
  its park after the click. Found, understood, left as the design's
  shape: the delivery is correct on any following event, and a
  silent machine has no lines to print anyway. (5) **The interactive
  halt** (2026-11-09, A16.5's first hand-driven session): the ride's
  machine froze at the client era with a real hand clicking the cocoa
  window — the press's own composition (T10.2) walks and flushes
  inside the notice handler, a whole-mode repair stretching that
  flush to hundreds of milliseconds, and the client era landing its
  presents inside one overran the stash T8.6 set four deep ("nobody's
  era holds four window presents inside one flush" — the interactive
  hand is somebody's). The stash is twelve deep now, its cells moved
  past the hidden words to +3264 (the first cut of the twelve ran
  +440..+536 and would have written the frame callbacks' pids over —
  the address-overlap lesson's third bite in two days); two fresh
  golden rounds green, the sums unchanged.
- **The golden.** The menu's open, the maximize (×5, the work area's
  whole width), the restore's menu at the carried seat, the restore,
  the minimize's compose, the desktop open's, the menu-quit's session
  end — every comp line pinned with its sum, deterministic across
  rounds; the clicks named through the device axes' inverse. **The
  drag** (A16.5, T10.5, 2026-11-09): the bar beside the button arms
  the hand, the driver's move-at steps (type 5, the press-at's
  packing, pushed while the button holds and the axes move) move the
  window by the seat's own `win_move`, there and back, the two steps'
  comp lines pinned — and the drag's cells taught the map a lesson:
  the first cut landed on the input hold's seat zero (the desk drag's
  moves wrote axes into the hold's pid word, the reply-to-nobody the
  loud end), the second on the frame allocator's own head, free and
  total (the arenas gone before the first spawn); the cells live in
  the twenty-four bytes between the exit ring's tail and the
  allocator's page, and the map's comment names the neighbors. The
  pins: the fb gate green (151), `run.sh` green under both timer
  transports — four rounds green, three byte-identical on both (the
  fourth's only variance the launch-era prompt-vs-rise interleave

  scratch instruments (the debug heartbeats, the throwaway locks) are

## 2026-11-08 — The composition's cost, measured (emo-desktop M15 opened, A15.1)

- **The number D9's milestone rides.** A14.1 measured what an app
  pays to paint (the Surface's verbs, `fill` 19 ms a megapixel, an
  opaque 1080p repaint 30–40 ms on QEMU's TCG); what an animation
  actually rides is the composition — the walk over the whole stack
  plus the flush — and that had never been timed. The probe: a
  scratch windowed pass installed the disk ELFs' way (T8.21's path —
  the blob beside the boot image, the bounds parked at the handoff
  page's free tail `0x87002210`, the install line, `exec probe.elf`
  from the REPL), its beats bracketed in serial markers — `m pre`/`m
  post` around `win_move` (op 16, state alone, the reply before any
  composition) and `pre`/`post` around `present` (op 17, whose reply
  lands only after `comp_compose` returns and the flush completes;
  the compositor's own `comp` line lands between the markers) — and a
  host watcher stamping every serial line as it lands, the way A14.1
  timestamped the lines. Ten compositions and ten moves over the
  desktop's own stack (the wallpaper, the header, the dock, the
  probe's 384×256 window dead center). **The numbers**: a whole-screen
  composition **about 230 ms** (226–274, median 230.4); the full
  present round trip median 235 ms; the client's own `round_digest`
  about 4 ms; the mailbox round trip **6–17 µs** (the parity-refused
  odd move about 130 µs, the walk's even-x discipline the probe's
  step tripped on). The messaging is three to four orders under the
  walk: **the composition's cost is the walk and the flush**. The
  arithmetic on the number: about 2.2 M words walked in about 220 ms
  of bracket, **about 100 ns a word** on TCG — a whole-screen
  recomposition ~4 fps, no motion rides it; a 384×256 window's own
  damage (two rects a step, ~0.2 M words) projects about 20 ms plus a
  rect flush, roughly 30 fps within reach of damage rectangles. The
  projection is arithmetic on the measured per-word cost, not a
  measurement; the digest is no separate pass (the `comp` line's sum
  accumulates during the walk). **The measured tree carried the
  blend's first, misapplied form** — the same word counts and loop
  shapes as the corrected one, one add apart per blended pixel — the
  cost conclusion is unchanged.
- **The blend's division retired, and the identity's own trap.** The
  lever on record since A14.1 — `(v + 127) / 255 == ((v + 128) +
  ((v + 128) >> 8)) >> 8` — adopted at the four sites that still
  divided (`ui_fb.mix`, `ui_fb.cover`, the kernel's `comp_mix`,
  `comp_cover`): `+128` **replaces** the `+127`, no extra temp. The
  first application kept the `+127` *and* added the `+128` — the
  identity's `v` is the value before the `+127` — a bias of half a
  rounding step on most blended pixels, present on every target at
  once (the form is portable Emo). The goldens named it twice: the fb
  gate's corpus FAIL lines (lost to a truncated pipe read; the round
  first misread the gate as green — the lesson: the gate's verdict is
  its exit and its stderr, never a pipe's tail), and `run.sh`'s
  first-frame digest, unmissable — the metal's frame 2525495889
  against the corpus's 511863226, every boot. The diagnostic that
  pinned it: a scratch bare-metal probe computing both forms over the
  domain and counting mismatches — the standalone identity exact at
  65,026 values, the misapplied mix wrong at 2,432 of the sampled
  lattice (first at s=0, d=15, a=15: division 14, misapplied 15), the
  corrected mix and cover exact across the lattice. The metal's
  `frame` back at the corpus's number the moment the `+128` replaced
  the `+127`. Sixteen consumers' locks regenerated with the sources
  (the T8.20 lesson's shape, twice this round — the first
  regeneration pinned the misapplied form and had to move aside
  again).
- **Found on the way, fixed: the ride's stale center.** `start.sh`'s
  auto-ride still waited for `pointer 512 384` — the 1024×768-era
  center, stale since T9.1's mode — so every 1080p boot stalled the
  ride at the pointer beat and handed the windows to the hand. The
  beat waits for `pointer 960 540` now, the mode's own center; the
  scratch driver (the ride's beats verbatim) drove the whole chain to
  the desktop and through the probe's session on the corrected beat.
- **What the number decides next** (the milestone stays open): the
  first animation's subject and whether the compositor grows damage
  rectangles — EmoOS Step 10's candidate first task — are the
  decisions the measurement now informs; neither is taken here.
- **The damage decision taken (T10.1, the same day).** The number left
  no third way: a whole-screen recomposition at ~230 ms carries no
  motion, and the frame callback's rhythm is worthless if every beat
  costs one. The shape is the smallest honest thing: **one bounding
  box**, not a rect list — the drag-path over-approximation it implies
  (a window's walk between two compositions marks its two ends, the
  intermediate positions nothing ever showed) costs nothing until a
  drag composes per frame, and the frame callback's world composes
  per frame by construction. The marks sit at the ops that unmake or
  make pixels — the present's rect, the move's painted-plus-new, the
  raise's crossed range (the quiet case, the already-top toplevel,
  marks nothing — the desktop's own focus beats), the prune's dead —
  and they read **painted records kept beside the stack slots**, not
  the rows, because the row that holds a surface's size is the close's
  to clear first. The generation's first composition marks the whole
  mode: the full clear's era erasure is the first composition's, and
  after it the pixels outside the box are the last composition's own —
  which is why every screendump model in `run.sh` passes unchanged
  and only the comp line's sums moved (18 of the pinned literals; the
  full-mode compositions' — the first frame, the wallpaper's own
  presents, the desktop's refolds — identical by construction).
  **The find the model caught**: the raise copied the entry to the
  stack's top without its painted record, and the entry inherited the
  removed slot's stale one — a closed window's shadow ring survived
  its own death by one blend step, the client-era model's pixel at
  (23,25) reading (25,25,29) against (26,26,30). The stale-slot shape
  is the page-map's own history repeating (the dead cell's first seat
  was the stash's second slot once); the records now ride the raise
  the way the entries do. **The flush carries the box** — gpu op 3,
  the rect packed in the message's third word (T8.11's shape), the
  reply the result the await's word — the full-screen op 0 standing
  behind it unused. **The damage world, measured** (A15.1's probe on
  the scratch tree, the same beats): a window-sized composition
  **27–44 ms, median ~36** — the walk's ~0.2 M word-pairs plus the
  wallpaper's pixels inside the box plus a ~0.4 MB rect flush —
  against the whole-screen 230: **six times the frames, ~25 fps
  within reach of the first animation**, the projection's arithmetic
  honest. The mailbox at its microseconds; the ledger `frames 774 576`
  unchanged (the box is four words and a parallel array on the page
  the compositor already owned).
- **What the milestone still decides**: the first animation's subject
  — the rhythm and the budget are now both real (the tick, the frame
  callback, ~36 ms a window-sized frame); the desktop's half of M15
  names it.
- **The animation, and what it rode (A15.2, the same day).** The
  subject was never in doubt once the numbers stood — the app window's
  own open and close, the desktop's most ordinary motion, the beat
  every desktop's user knows. The shape: the window mounts a quarter
  of itself low with the landing frame the app's own hand paints —
  the mount's tree through a throwaway `ui.Registry`, whose tags
  register in the view's own order, the indices the loop's registry
  agrees with, the corpus's digest untouched — and `Win.rise` eases
  it home: the offset's square decaying, the metal's division
  truncating the model's way, the last move landing the final seat
  unpainted for the loop's mount (the mount's own present closes the
  rise, one frame fewer on the wire). `Win.sink` is the mirror easing
  in, every frame presented, the window a quarter low when
  `win_close` takes it. The rise is the window's own height by four,
  the steps eight — a size the window derives, a count the animation
  owns. **The pacing decision (D12)**: the frame callback cannot pace
  a solo animation. Its park answers at the next composition's
  completion, and the only composer is the parked client itself — a
  park before the first present waits on itself, the deadlock the
  semantics as designed produce. The animation rides the present's
  own reply (op 17's lands after the walk and the flush), each frame
  the damage rectangle's budget; the callback's seat stays what
  T8.15 proved — a client that has already presented reads the next
  completion, the multi-client's cadence instrument. **The race the
  golden had to name**: the launch era runs three clients at once —
  the app's rise, the dock's lit tile, the header's count — and a
  composition's box is whoever's marks are pending: the editor's move
  landed inside the dock's pending box, the dock's sum became the
  scheduler's own, differing across the transports at the same beat.
  The golden pins those beats by position alone (the box's words are
  nobody's contract), keeps the slide's own sums (single-client,
  identical across both transports), and the three rounds' serials
  vary exactly there — A14.1's recorded race's shape, the assertions
  indifferent. **The mid-flight screendump was tried and retired**:
  the await's tenth-of-a-second poll and the monitor's
  hundred-millisecond dump against frames ten to forty milliseconds
  apart — the dump lands frames late, and the wire's comp lines, the
  position and the box's words pinned per frame, are the intermediate
  frames' record the dump cannot outpace. The pins: `run.sh` green
  under both timer transports three consecutive rounds, the slide's
  sums identical across both, the variance only at the unpinned
  launch-era pair; the full `dune runtest` green; `@fmt` green; the
  fb gate green, the winbuf's five consumer locks regenerated. **M15
  complete.**

## 2026-11-07 — The windows (emo-desktop A14.5, M14 complete)

- **The shared title bar.** One bar every window wears: the three
  traffic-light buttons (the shapes' card's own three circles — the
  red, the yellow, the green when the window holds the focus, one
  quiet gray when it does not), the title in the interface face
  centered on the bar's width, the light papers 0xFFF4F4F6 and
  0xFFE6E6EA with the hairline at the foot. The painter has the two
  states and the shell's windows live in both — the window corpus's
  session pins focused and unfocused alike.
- **The Chrome.** The apps' views ride under it: a content surface
  whose every paint lands `bar_height()` lower on the real buffer, the
  bar painted once at the mounting, the Elm loop seeing a surface of
  the content's own size — the counter, the browser and the editor
  reskinned without their views knowing a window exists. The state the
  apps' bars wear is the launch's own: the machine focuses the new
  window's reader (the keys reach it before any click), and no wire
  yet tells the app when the focus moves on — the dock's lit tile
  carries the same gap (A12.2's record). The day the wire exists, the
  bar has the other state waiting.
- **The antialiased pointer.** The grid speaks the font's sixteen
  levels of coverage (A14.2's alphabet), the partial cells the
  outline's ink at its own coverage — the body's paper always whole
  inside the outline. The upload writes `(coverage << 24) | ink`, the
  compositor's plane blends (T9.2's source-over), and the model of the
  display blends the same words over its own background arithmetic —
  shadow rings included — so the screendump's every partial cell is
  the model's to verify.
- **Finds.** The upload's shift is the coverage's own, not the alpha's:
  `0xFF000000 + coverage` put the coverage in the ink's blue lane and
  the corners of the cursor painted opaque — the model caught it at
  the first walk, the shift constants written whole. The models'
  sprite sentinel is a word, not a pair — the `(None, 0)` tuple's
  unpack was the TypeError the first walk died of.
- Pins: the window and apps corpora re-pinned on the interpreter and
  all five hosted targets (the fb gate green), `run.sh` under both
  transports three rounds, `dune runtest` green with the smoke,
  `@fmt` green. **M14 complete** — the HD desktop's arc, A14.1
  through A14.5 with EmoOS Step 9 beside it.

## 2026-11-07 — The chrome, the corners, the close (emo-desktop A14.4, EmoOS T9.2+T9.3)

- **The chrome is the wallpaper's own formula under a scrim.** D8's
  material, made: the header and the dock recompute the wallpaper's
  art at their rows — the gradient and every ribbon whose span reaches
  them — under the panel's scrim (the header's 0xB41E1E26, the dock's
  0xB424242C), row by row, three blends a row. The panel reads the
  formula and never the pixels behind it, so the material is what the
  chrome always believed the wallpaper was — a window sliding under
  the dock changes nothing the dock shows (the standing note D8 made,
  now the walk's topmost strip's actual behavior). The wallpaper's own
  art is untouched: its digest `wall 1307018457` is the number A13
  pinned.
- **The dock is a shelf.** A translucent slab (6, 3, w−12, 34) at
  radius 12 floats on the material — glass over the wallpaper's own
  colors, graded 0x66484C5C down to 0x4C34363E — its soft shadow
  (8 pixels, 0x80000000) beneath it, and the tiles drawn icons on it:
  rounded squares 32×32 at radius 8, graded top to bottom (the focused
  app's lit steel 0xFF7090B8→0xFF4A6288, the quiet tiles
  0xFF50586A→0xFF3A4150), the app's initial centered in the interface
  face, the running dot at the foot. The tiles start 20 pixels in — the
  shelf's corner arcs own the first pixels — and the desktop's document
  and folder icons are drawn the same way: the page a graded rounded
  square with its folded corner and three line pills, the folder's tab
  and graded body, the selection a rounded translucent card. No image
  assets anywhere; the face is the shapes' own.
- **The metal's painting stayed typed.** The chrome's repaint runs
  through typed twins of the package's painters — `desk_chrome_row`
  (the material's row: the gradient's fill, the ribbon's blend, the
  scrim's blend), `desk_bar_paint`, `desk_dock_paint`,
  `desk_strip_paint` — every call the carve's own (`carve_fill`,
  `carve_blend`, `carve_shadow`, `carve_rounded`, `carve_ink`), no
  interface call in a repaint, the A13 rule held: a class body's
  boxing never comes back, and the dock repaints for a living.
- **The round's find: the corner blend's alpha.** The masked blit's
  partial pixels blended the window's word at its coverage —
  `(c << 24) | v` — and the screendump's corner pixels came back "too
  content" by an alpha no single coverage explained. Two kernel debug
  prints settled it: the shadow's value under the corner was the
  model's own, the corner's coverage and word were the model's own,
  and the stored result was the model's over a *different* alpha —
  the window word's own top byte, the client's to keep (the window
  era's pattern rides `0x57` there), OR-ed into the coverage: 97 |
  0x57 = 0x77 = 119. The fix is the mask: `(c << 24) | (v &
  0xFFFFFF)` — the coverage replaces the client's byte, never ORs it.
  The walk's own reproach: the model had the pixel right all along.
- **The second find: the shadow's corner spans.** The shadow's pass
  painted the left corner zones on the top rows and the right ones on
  the bottom rows — the top-right and bottom-left corners' shadows
  were missing, the slate showing where the ramp belonged. Both corner
  zones on every corner row; the model caught it before any eye did.
- **The folds are the window's own pixels.** A window's rectangle on
  the screen now shows the background at its corners, so the corpus's
  window digests became round folds — `round_digest(s, 10)` in the
  hosted twins, the corner squares set aside — and the metal's frame
  lines print the same fold over their buffers. The boot image's own
  `Win` was the third site: the shell and the desk pass predate the
  winbuf package, so the package's `present` fix alone changed nothing
  on the metal — the boot image carries its own class, its present's
  digest its own site. (The desk-era frames now match the corpus's
  round lines; the debug print that proved it — the image's direct
  `round_digest` answering the corpus's number while the class's
  `present` answered the old full one — is the record's receipt.)
  `run.sh`'s folds skip the same squares by the window's *local* row —
  the first fold tested the screen's row and, for a window at y=128,
  every row was "past the bottom corner" — and the dock's strip keeps
  the plain fold: the walk masks toplevels only, and the panel's
  digest is the panel's own.
- **The neighbors' shadows.** The quadrant windows at `(mw − 384) / 4`
  put the editor's right edge exactly at the counter's left edge, and
  the shadow's nine-pixel ring fell inside the counter's fold — the
  corpus's canvas cannot know a neighbor's shadow. The placement yields
  the shadow its reach: `(mw − 384) / 4 − 10`, the ring's last pixel
  one short of the neighbor. macOS windows shade each other; golden
  windows keep their distance.
- **T9.3: the close's other half.** The gate stages the caller's
  space word with the close (as `win_new`'s does), and verb 14 unmaps
  the run's leaves from the owner's table *before* the blocks go back
  — `space.unmap_run` walks the exact sequence `map_range` mapped by,
  every leaf checked a leaf of the very page taken back (the mapping
  is the identity of its physical addresses, so the walk's every leaf
  is one the map wrote; anything else is the loud end), the
  translations fenced after. The golden beat is the desk pass's `c`:
  beta closed and another window opened in the same life, the fresh
  map riding the very blocks the close gave back — pre-fix, the
  machine died `sv39: an address mapped twice` right there; post-fix
  the reopen composes, the new grant's `desk b 10` on the wire. The
  reopen's own find: the stack entry is made at the role verb, not at
  `win_new` — a reopened window that skips `win_role` has no place on
  the stack, and its present composes nothing; the beat declares the
  role.
- **The re-pin.** Every composition's sum changed (the shadows' words
  in, the masked corners' out; the chrome's content; the drawn
  icons): the comp lines re-pinned from the machine's own wire, the
  ledger `frames 771 573` → `frames 774 576` (the images' new weight),
  the desk-era comp ordering re-mapped (the rm's composition now lands
  before the REPL's next refusals — the pacing moved with the
  content).
- Pins: `run.sh` under both transports three rounds; the fb gate
  green (the dock's strip, the bar, the icons' goldens re-pinned on
  the interpreter and all five hosted targets); `dune runtest` 371
  green with the smoke; `@fmt` green.

## 2026-11-07 — The mode (emo-desktop A14.3, EmoOS T9.1)

- **The budget question answered on the machine.** The mode's demands
  were known (the wallpaper's window buffer 48 → 127 blocks, the
  header's and the dock's with it, +82 the desktop's own; the carve
  8 MiB = 128 blocks grown downward off the region's top); what the
  paper could not say was whether the region still held the desktop
  plus three residents. The machine answered at the editor's exec —
  `desktop: the kernel is out of a resource`, the take_run that could
  not find its 102 contiguous blocks. The disk ELFs' round (A11.1)
  had spent the kernel heap 96 → 64 MiB to grow the region for the
  three residents; the mode spent the region again, and **the round's
  lever is the same one: the kernel heap 64 → 32 MiB**. The heap's
  live users are the root's bump (a few KiB) and the const region at
  its top — the hooks serve every spawned pass from the region, so
  the bare-metal spawn's carve the heap once backed is dead code —
  and 512 blocks come back. No `-m` (the memory map's top stays
  `0x88000000`, QEMU's 128 MiB default holding it exactly), no second
  region, no allocator surgery. The dune pin (`.zero 67108864`) moved
  with it. The trio now fits with room beside it, and the ledger's
  arithmetic says so: `frames 685 569` → `771 573` (+86 taken, +4
  given — the desktop's own +82 are live surfaces at poweroff, never
  given).
- **The art scales, the design constants stay.** The wallpaper's
  gradient already derived from h; its three ribbons were tuned at
  1024×768 with raw pixel constants. The landed form expresses every
  band's rows, offsets and widths as fractions of w and h whose
  denominators (256, 128, 24, 16, 8, 192) reproduce the tuned values
  exactly at 1024×768 — `(5·h)/8` is 480 there, `(115·w)/256` is 460 —
  and stretch them at the mode. The slopes stay the art's own, and
  the truncation discipline held: the metal's typed mirror, the
  hosted canvas and `run.sh`'s model divide the same way, and the
  launch-era and desktop-era screendumps model to the pixel at
  1920×1080. The chrome's own sizes — the 24px header, the 40px dock,
  the 96×76 icon cells — are the face's design, not the mode's; A14.4
  redraws them.
- **The places derive; the proofs keep theirs.** The apps read the
  mode through `output_info` and place their windows — the counter
  dead center, the browser and editor its quadrants. The panel proof
  (T8.15's full-width strip) takes the mode's own width at the
  display's bottom edge. The desk pass's two demo windows keep their
  absolute places (they are the drag proof's fixtures, and the drag
  targets with them), and every `abs` beat's device value was
  recomputed for the new axes with the compositor's own scale checked
  (`floor(v·mode/32768) = pixel`).
- **Two finds in the pins.** The bare comp pins (`comp 576 384`) had
  been prefix-matching the compositor's summed lines all along — the
  desk window's mount and the counter's were never bare — so the
  remap split them by position in the list, and the comment that said
  they "shared" the line now says why they stopped. The screendump
  header `P6\n1920 1080\n255\n` is 17 bytes where the 768p one was
